## Corpus Check
- 1626 files · ~2,351,788 words

## Summary
- 34624 nodes · 70744 edges · 1364 communities (1168 shown, 196 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6952 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9c750729`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
